      A B →
    @IsUnit.{0} (ExactLiouvilleComparison.NormalizationAlgebra.Mat n)
        (@Semiring.toMonoid.{0} (ExactLiouvilleComparison.NormalizationAlgebra.Mat n)
          (@Matrix.semiring.{0, 0} (Fin n) Real Real.semiring (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
        B →
      @Commute.{0} (ExactLiouvilleComparison.NormalizationAlgebra.Mat n)
        (@Matrix.instMulOfFintypeOfAddCommMonoid.{0, 0} (Fin n) Real (Fin.fintype n) Real.instMul
          Real.instAddCommMonoid)
        A

          (@Matrix.inv.{0, 0} (Fin n) Real (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) Real.commRing) B)
